# Results board

Generated from `cards/*.json` by `tools/board.py`. Do not edit. Verified cards are listed before unverified ones. Latency values are cycles per call (ratio = control over variant, above 1 is a win), throughput values are Gelem/s (ratio = variant over control). Every value carries its control from the same lock section, see the card for canaries, resources and findings.
